# Sojourn's sounds: <sound id> = <file under E:/Overwatch Archive/extracted/154088/> (build 154088). Same format as
# sojourn.txt / brigitte.txt; build_asset_pack.py sounds copies / mixes them into the local pack. Nothing here has been
# listened to. Read 2026-11-02 offline (Overwatch and Battle.net checked closed before every archive read and DataTool /
# TankLib run): her 02C sounds' Wwise banks decoded exactly (ww.py, dump_banks.py, emote_events.py / wwfull.py from
# research/emote-sounds/scripts: children from each object's parent id, random / sequence / switch / layer parameters,
# volume, make-up gain, pitch, delays, the events' actions, RTPC curves), the Init bank's bus volumes and game parameter
# defaults (1E818, stmg.py). Scripts: E:/Overwatch Archive/research/sojourn/scripts/sounds/ (sojourn_gen.py writes this
# file from sojourn_table.py; Juno's pipeline copied and retargeted); their data (decode sojourn_ww.json, bank dump
# banks_raw.json, measurements sojourn_measure.json, her entities' effects fx_proj.json, contact sets contacts.json, voice
# lines voice_lines.json, the RTPC defaults rtpc_defaults.json, one block per 02C overview.txt): research/sojourn/sounds/.
# Effect and clip timings: research/sojourn/work/fx_sounds_timed.json (fx_sounds2_sojourn.py over work/graph_effects.json)
# and work/clip_sounds.txt; graph nodes: research/sojourn/work_kit decompiles (fx_nodes.py: work/fx_nodes_out*.txt) and
# kit_sojourn.md; the kit itself: tools/reference/sojourn-map.md; this map read for wiring: tools/reference/sojourn-sounds.md.
# The live kit: Railgun (fire, projectile, hits, reload, energy), Charged Shot, Power Slide (and its jump), Disruptor Shot
# (cast, flight, hits, field, burn), Overclock; her live perks (Dual Thrusters' sounds), the Railgun's idle hum, the 1P
# energy-state cues and quick melee foley as EXTRA. The Stadium powers' sounds are left out ('Not mapped').
# Files: her skin and voice extraction are in research/sojourn/extract, not under the build root, so those paths reach it
# through ../../research/sojourn/extract/; the 36 banks it lacked (contact sets, projectile slots, the slide's contacts, the
# Dual Thrusters copies) were extracted for this map into research/sojourn/sounds/ExtraSounds/Sojourn (extract_banks.py,
# DataTool's own sound export; manifest.json there: RailgunProjectile, RailgunContact, ChargedShot, ChargedShotContact,
# PowerSlide, DisruptorContact, OverclockContact (Stadium, not used)), reached through
# ../../research/sojourn/sounds/ExtraSounds/Sojourn/. Ids: 02C sounds (643C = 00000000643C.02C), 00D effects, 006 clips,
# 08F clip animation effects, 049 contact sets; graphs 01B with their node numbers. Confidence: H read in the data, M
# inferred, L guess.
# Gains: Wwise volume + make-up gain summed from each file up through its containers and mixers (the middle of a random
# range included) plus its output bus chain, plus RTPC curves (dB scaling 20*log10(1+y); linear between points) and
# SetVolume actions. RTPCs are read at their game parameter's default (the Init bank's list); modulators: an LFO at its
# middle, a time modulator at its start on one-shots and at its end on loops. As in sojourn.txt, ONE offset per family keeps
# her sounds' balance with each other, bringing the family's loudest single layer to -1 dBFS (the builder's limiter catches
# sums): own -0.5 dB (loudest: sojourn.disruptor.hit), 3p +0.5 dB (loudest: sojourn.disruptor.hit_3p).
# Pitch: cents as a speed factor (*). Low / high-pass filters, random pitch / volume per play, fades, Seek, Wwise states
# and the voice priority RTPCs are not reproduced. Layers more than 60 dB down are left out, and in loops those 20 dB
# under the loudest loop. A loop's LOOP LENGTH is its longest bed within 6 dB of the loudest layer; shorter layers are
# tiled across it and beds more than 15 % longer are left out. The Railgun's fire is a self-repeating stream (2BF5's
# trigger-rate containers, as Sombra's pistol): built per shot (fire_first, fire) with its ring-out and Stop sets as
# fire_end.
# Own vs others: her STUSounds with audience 1 (643C, 517B, 5185, 549E, 54E9, 54EB, 9210, 5ECD) are read as hers (H by
# Lucio's pairs; 54EB and 9210 carry a range because they sound at the projectile / field, Sombra's lesson); the other
# no-range copies (audience 2) are read as the player the effect plays on (M, Juno's / Brigitte's reading): 'own' is
# Sojourn, or the enemy burning in her field where the rule says. Ranged copies are everyone else's ('3p'); where an effect
# or contact holds two ranged copies with the same files they are read as the two teams' (or near / far) copies (L which):
# the louder is built, the other noted. Effect sounds with no own copy (the projectiles in flight, contact hits on the
# world) are built once for everyone ('both'). Ranges come through the volume (sounds.md).

# ===== How each game event is put together (rules)
# sojourn.railgun.fire_first: Railgun first shot, own: 2BF5's 643C (no range, audience 1: hers, H): first-shot sets (a
#  0.07 s report, a random 5 of 0.08 s) once and one trigger of the stream sets (a random 38 of 0.07-0.08 s, a 0.06 s
#  click at -1040 cents, a random 20 of 0.03-0.05 s at +200, a 0.63 s body at -480). Built per shot, as Sombra's
#  pistol: one trigger of each stream container for the game to play with each shot (M); the 2.8-3.1 s ring-out
#  container goes to fire_end, else 16 tails a second pile up.
# Trigger: PRIMARY held (1A23 #527 -> #526 false -> #571 StateStack holds #639 effect 2BF5 on the gun at the muzzle hp
#  0006, H); 2BF5's 02C are self-repeating streams (trigger-rate containers paced by the fire-rate parameter
#  4066808683, 0.071 s at its default 100, H; her 16 shots a second set by the volley #502, M that the game drives the
#  parameter); the first shot of a burst: the stream containers plus the two once-only first-shot containers.
# Layers: a 643C 1005483600 RanSeq 0.07-0.08 s x38, net -1.5; b 643C 828902198 RanSeq 0.03-0.05 s x20 +200 cents, net
#  -23; c 643C 762124351 RanSeq 0.08 s x5, net -0.5; d 643C 872527193 RanSeq 0.63 s -480 cents, net -21; e 643C
#  278722623 RanSeq 0.06 s -1040 cents, net -9.5; f 643C 702687221 RanSeq 0.07 s, net -0.5.
# Left out: 643C 739596218 (-116.5 dB), 643C 920023181 (-109 dB), 643C 1451582 (-133 dB), 643C 118602741 (-108 dB),
#  643C 978972974 (-117 dB).
@sojourn.railgun.fire_first = sojourn.railgun.fire_first.a@0.01:-2 + sojourn.railgun.fire_first.b*1.122:-23.5 + sojourn.railgun.fire_first.c@0.01:-1 + sojourn.railgun.fire_first.d*0.758:-21.5 + sojourn.railgun.fire_first.e*0.548@0.01:-10 + sojourn.railgun.fire_first.f@0.01:-1
# sojourn.railgun.fire: Railgun shot, own: one trigger of 643C's stream containers (the once-only first-shot sets left
#  to fire_first). Built per shot, as Sombra's pistol: one trigger of each stream container for the game to play with
#  each shot (M); the 2.8-3.1 s ring-out container goes to fire_end, else 16 tails a second pile up.
# Trigger: PRIMARY held (1A23 #527 -> #526 false -> #571 StateStack holds #639 effect 2BF5 on the gun at the muzzle hp
#  0006, H); 2BF5's 02C are self-repeating streams (trigger-rate containers paced by the fire-rate parameter
#  4066808683, 0.071 s at its default 100, H; her 16 shots a second set by the volley #502, M that the game drives the
#  parameter); every later shot of the burst (16 a second).
# Layers: a 643C 1005483600 RanSeq 0.07-0.08 s x38, net -1.5; b 643C 828902198 RanSeq 0.03-0.05 s x20 +200 cents, net
#  -23; c 643C 872527193 RanSeq 0.63 s -480 cents, net -21; d 643C 278722623 RanSeq 0.06 s -1040 cents, net -9.5.
@sojourn.railgun.fire = sojourn.railgun.fire.a@0.01:-2 + sojourn.railgun.fire.b*1.122:-23.5 + sojourn.railgun.fire.c*0.758:-21.5 + sojourn.railgun.fire.d*0.548@0.01:-10
# sojourn.railgun.fire_end: Railgun burst tail, own: 643C's ring-out container (a random 7 of 2.77-3.06 s at -334
#  cents; in the game one per repeat, layered) and its Stop-event sets (a random 4 of 3.33-3.59 s at +250, a random 4
#  of 2.18-2.21 s).
# Trigger: the burst ends (PRIMARY let go, the magazine empty or a state taking the gun: #571 ends and 2BF5 stops;
#  643C's Stop event plays its two tail sets, H).
# Layers: a 643C 924656042 RanSeq 2.77-3.06 s x7 -334 cents, net -17; b 643C Stop-event 221593317 RanSeq 3.33-3.59 s
#  x4 +250 cents, net -13.5; c 643C Stop-event 758907773 RanSeq 2.18-2.21 s x4 +35 cents, net -7.5.
@sojourn.railgun.fire_end = sojourn.railgun.fire_end.a*0.825:-17.5 + sojourn.railgun.fire_end.b*1.155:-14 + sojourn.railgun.fire_end.c*1.020:-8
# sojourn.railgun.fire_first_3p: Railgun first shot heard by others: 8AF0 (100 m): the first-shot sets once and one
#  trigger of the stream sets.
# Trigger: the same first shot, at her for everyone else: 2BF5's 100 m copy 8AF0 (audience 2; the same containers as
#  643C 5-11 dB lower, H).
# Layers: a 8AF0 658303913 RanSeq 0.07-0.08 s x38, net -6.5; b 8AF0 780566627 RanSeq 0.03-0.05 s x20 +200 cents, net
#  -19; c 8AF0 628617853 RanSeq 0.08 s x5, net -5.5; d 8AF0 449085178 RanSeq 0.06 s -1040 cents, net -20.5; e 8AF0
#  1064806900 RanSeq 0.07 s, net -6.5.
# Left out: 8AF0 218755352 (-118 dB), 8AF0 279930316 (-123.5 dB).
@sojourn.railgun.fire_first_3p = sojourn.railgun.fire_first_3p.a@0.01:-6 + sojourn.railgun.fire_first_3p.b*1.122:-18.5 + sojourn.railgun.fire_first_3p.c@0.01:-5 + sojourn.railgun.fire_first_3p.d*0.548@0.01:-20 + sojourn.railgun.fire_first_3p.e@0.01:-6
# sojourn.railgun.fire_3p: Railgun shot heard by others: one trigger of 8AF0's stream containers (8AF0 has no 0.63 s body).
# Trigger: the same later shots, at her for everyone else.
# Layers: a 8AF0 658303913 RanSeq 0.07-0.08 s x38, net -6.5; b 8AF0 780566627 RanSeq 0.03-0.05 s x20 +200 cents, net
#  -19; c 8AF0 449085178 RanSeq 0.06 s -1040 cents, net -20.5.
@sojourn.railgun.fire_3p = sojourn.railgun.fire_3p.a@0.01:-6 + sojourn.railgun.fire_3p.b*1.122:-18.5 + sojourn.railgun.fire_3p.c*0.548@0.01:-20
# sojourn.railgun.fire_end_3p: Railgun burst tail heard by others: 8AF0's ring-out container and its Stop-event sets.
# Trigger: the burst ends, at her for everyone else (8AF0's Stop event plays the same two tail sets, H).
# Layers: a 8AF0 135938942 RanSeq 2.77-3.06 s x7 -334 cents, net -24; b 8AF0 Stop-event 657747809 RanSeq 3.33-3.59 s
#  x4 +250 cents, net -26.5; c 8AF0 Stop-event 792393476 RanSeq 2.18-2.21 s x4 +35 cents, net -20.5.
@sojourn.railgun.fire_end_3p = sojourn.railgun.fire_end_3p.a*0.825:-23.5 + sojourn.railgun.fire_end_3p.b*1.155:-26 + sojourn.railgun.fire_end_3p.c*1.020:-20
# sojourn.railgun.projectile: Railgun projectile in flight: 3412's 8F0D (60 m, audience 2: a random 14 of 0.28-0.64 s

# sojourn.railgun.hit_enemy: Railgun hit on an enemy, own: 8D36 (no range, audience 2: a random 8 of 0.28-0.30 s at
#  +200 cents, a random 7 of 0.13-0.31 s at +200 and +900 0.035 s late, a random 10 of 0.18-0.46 s). Not in her
#  extraction (ExtraSounds/Sojourn/RailgunContact).
# Trigger: a railgun projectile hits an enemy: contact set 0701 type 0, material 0001 -> contact effect 3E84 -> 8D36
#  (no range), read as the shooter's (M: type 0 = characters, the no-range copy hers).
# Layers: a 8D36 870881486 RanSeq 0.18-0.46 s x10, net 0; b 8D36 923138318 RanSeq 0.28-0.30 s x8 +200 cents, net -7.5;

# sojourn.charged.fire: Charged Shot, own: 2835's 517B (no range, audience 1: hers, H): a random 4 of 2.90-3.12 s at

# sojourn.charged.fire_3p: Charged Shot heard by others: 517D (100 m: a random 3 of 1.83-2.09 s, the 0.52 s and 0.13 s
#  cracks, the random 4 of 2.23-3.02 s). Built in three bins of the parameter 2139477060 (0-1; M: the energy spent,
#  v87 / 100), as Lifeweaver's blossom: full 1.0 (energy over 90), _mid 0.6 (30-90), _low 0.15 (30 or less):
#  OcSounds.byCharge.

# ===== Voice (HeroVoice/Sojourn): the voice set 01C2.05F's lines by moment (research/sojourn/sounds/voice_lines.json,
# from sojourn_voice.py: each line's 0B2, stimulus, weight and decoded criteria; files from DataTool's extract-hero-voice
# --subtitles-with-sounds, in research/sojourn/extract). w = the line's weight (the builder turns it into Minecraft
# weights). Lines with criteria are left out, as Juno's and Brigitte's are (hero interactions, skins, maps, Overwatch
# Classic, scripted moments: counted per moment), except 'NOT ScriptedEvent 191E', treated as always true (kiriko-voice.md),
# and the two lines named in their moments. Stimulus numbers (the .078): priority, radius m, volume dB, interrupt,
# cooldown s (m_A73F2EBE-m_DD27AC95 read as min-max, M); Voice.java's table takes priority, cooldown and interrupt. Her set

# ===== For the integration: each event, loop or one-shot, who it is built for, its length, and what triggers it
# (own = the player the effect plays on, in the head: Sojourn, or the ally where the rule says; 3p = everyone else, at her
# / the target / the hit point; both = one rule for everyone). 'EXTRA' = in the data but not needed for the kit's
# moments. Not built: see 'Not mapped'.
#   sojourn.railgun.fire_first         one-shot own  0.83 s; PRIMARY held (1A23 #527 -> #526 false -> #571 StateStack
#     holds #639 effect 2BF5 on the gun at the muzzle hp 0006, H); 2BF5's 02C are self-repeating streams (trigger-rate
#     containers paced by the fire-rate parameter 4066808683, 0.071 s at its default 100, H; her 16 shots a second set
#     by the volley #502, M that the game drives the parameter); the first shot of a burst: the stream containers plus
#     the two once-only first-shot containers
#   sojourn.railgun.fire               one-shot own  0.83 s; PRIMARY held (1A23 #527 -> #526 false -> #571 StateStack
#     holds #639 effect 2BF5 on the gun at the muzzle hp 0006, H); 2BF5's 02C are self-repeating streams (trigger-rate
#     containers paced by the fire-rate parameter 4066808683, 0.071 s at its default 100, H; her 16 shots a second set
#     by the volley #502, M that the game drives the parameter); every later shot of the burst (16 a second)
#   sojourn.railgun.fire_end           one-shot own  3.72 s; the burst ends (PRIMARY let go, the magazine empty or a
#     state taking the gun: #571 ends and 2BF5 stops; 643C's Stop event plays its two tail sets, H)
#   sojourn.railgun.fire_first_3p      one-shot 3p   0.11 s; the same first shot, at her for everyone else: 2BF5's 100
#     m copy 8AF0 (audience 2; the same containers as 643C 5-11 dB lower, H)
#   sojourn.railgun.fire_3p            one-shot 3p   0.11 s; the same later shots, at her for everyone else
#   sojourn.railgun.fire_end_3p        one-shot 3p   3.72 s; the burst ends, at her for everyone else (8AF0's Stop
#     event plays the same two tail sets, H)
#   sojourn.railgun.projectile         one-shot both 0.65 s; each railgun projectile as it is made: 2F98's
#     STUProjectileVisualComponent m_2170A015 'sound only' slot, effect 3412 (H slot; Juno's / Ana's reading of the
#     slot, M); 3412 holds 603E and 8F0D, the same 14 files at 60 m (8F0D 1 dB louder: L which team's; the louder
#     built)
#   sojourn.railgun.hit                one-shot own  0.48 s; a railgun projectile hits a character: 2F98's m_A18274A3
#     'on a target' effect 2837 holds sub-effect 4010 -> 8DE7 (H), the shooter's shared hit crack (M, Ana's / Juno's
#     reading)
#   sojourn.railgun.hit_enemy          one-shot own  0.46 s; a railgun projectile hits an enemy: contact set 0701 type
#     0, material 0001 -> contact effect 3E84 -> 8D36 (no range), read as the shooter's (M: type 0 = characters, the
#     no-range copy hers)

#     9058 (500 m; 9054 is 12 m, the same files 7 dB quieter: L near / far; 9058 built)
#   sojourn.railgun.hit_block          one-shot both 0.84 s; a railgun projectile hits the world: 0701 type 2,
#     material 0001 -> 3E90 -> 5F13 (90 m) + 5F21 (32 m), both on the contact effect (H; built together as Juno's 899F
#     + 89A1, M)
#   sojourn.railgun.reload             one-shot own  1.34 s; RELOAD (1A23 #80 when the magazine is not full, or empty:
#     #1133) -> #144 alias 0x004: the Railgun's **1P clip 6FCB** (1.3 s) animation effect 30A6: 921C at 0, 921D at
#     0.566, 921E at 0.812, 921F at 1.106 s (H); her reload is 1.2 s (the clip plays at 1.3 / 1.2 speed: M, the times
#     above at the clip's own rate)
#   sojourn.railgun.reload_3p          one-shot 3p   1.34 s; the same reload for everyone else: the Railgun's **3P
#     clip 70D5** effect 30C4: 962A-962D (45 m) at the same times (H); 30C4 also holds the 1P set 921C-921F at
#     slightly other times (0, 0.567, 0 (held 1.3 s), 0.813: L, a copy for a spectating view): not built
#   sojourn.railgun.idle.loop (EXTRA)  LOOP     own  loop 11.719 s; held from the weapon's start: 1A23's init stack
#     (#1509 / #1549 onBegin) -> #1172 -> #624 effect 28EB on her (hp 00A4), held while the Railgun is (H nodes; M: an

# The shared moments use the shared stimuli (the .078 are global) and Voice.java's shared table already holds their
# numbers. Overclock's press raises 185C (own, key F1AC), 0B19 (allies, F1AB) and 0D2F (enemies, F1AA) together (2159 #363
# -> #771): with ultLine playing 0B19 / 0D2F, 'ultimate' (185C) is her own copy of the ENEMY line's text ('This ends now!'),
# not the friendly one's (Juno's 184D copies her friendly line): pick one. The Charged Shot with no energy plays the shared
# 'ability not available' click (0186 -> 0FCB): kiriko.ability.denied.
